   278→### Phase 2: Code Quality (P2) — 5 Parallel Splits, ~3,500 tokens

   280→> **GATE from Phase 1:** `uv run pytest tests/ -q --tb=no` → 0 failures.

   282→**Common pattern for all splits:**
   283→1. Read the file, identify logical clusters (endpoint groups, utility functions, class hierarchies)
   284→2. Extract each cluster into a focused module (≤300 LOC)
   285→3. Re-export from the original module for backward compatibility
   286→4. Run affected tests after each split to catch regressions immediately

   419→## Agent Team Assignments
   420→
   421→> **Compound principle:** Specialist agents handle their domain. Parallel execution where no dependencies exist. Each agent's output feeds the next phase's input.
   422→
   423→### Phase 0: Foundation Team (1 agent, sequential)
   424→
   425→| Agent | Role | Tasks | Estimated Tokens |
   426→|-------|------|-------|-----------------|
   427→| **Package Surgeon** | Import/package fixes | T1, T2 | 500 |
   428→
   429→### Phase 1: Test Stability Team (3 agents, parallel)
   430→
   431→| Agent | Role | Tasks | Estimated Tokens |
   432→|-------|------|-------|-----------------|
   433→| **Config Test Specialist** | Pre-commit/security test assertions | T3 | 1,200 |
   434→| **Swarm Test Specialist** | Async mock and retry test fixes | T4 | 800 |

   454→
   455→## Testing Strategy
   456→
   457→- **Per-task:** Run affected test file after each task (`uv run pytest tests/<module>/ -q --tb=short`)
   458→- **Per-phase gate:** Run full suite (`uv run pytest tests/ -q --tb=no`) at each gate
   459→- **Final validation:** Full suite + import check + `uv run python src/cohezion/healing/deep_audit.py`
   460→
   461→## Risks and Mitigations
   462→
   463→| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
   464→|------|-----------|--------|------------|
   465→| File split breaks imports | Medium | High | Re-export from original module; run tests after each split |
   466→| Pre-commit tests have other hidden assertions | Low | Low | Read full test file before fixing; run full security test suite |
   467→| Token client tests are order-dependent | Medium | Medium | Add conftest fixture to reset client state; test in isolation AND full suite |
   468→| Executor split breaks 11-step pipeline | Medium | High | Read executor.py completely before splitting; verify step order preserved |
   469→| Skill wiring breaks coherence check | Low | Medium | Set conservative coherence threshold (0.7); monitor first compound cycle |
   470→
   471→## Compound Engineering Value
   472→
   473→Each phase's output compounds into the next:
   474→- **Phase 0** unblocks git health analysis → informs Phase 1 test fixes
   475→- **Phase 1** creates a green test suite → safe base for Phase 2 refactoring
   476→- **Phase 2** splits reduce complexity → enables Phase 3 skill wiring in clean modules
   477→- **Phase 3** wires skills → increases compound loop coverage → future cycles improve faster
   478→
   479→**HIHO impact:** Phases 0-2 are structural (no coherence impact). Phase 3 increases skill coverage from 97.7% to 100%, expanding the compound loop's effective domain.
   480→

    23→def run_async_guardrail(coro: Any) -> Any:
    24→    """Execute async guardrail check in sync context.
    25→
    26→    Non-blocking on failure - logs and returns None.
    27→
    28→    Args:
    29→        coro: Async coroutine to execute
    30→
    31→    Returns:

    33→    """
    34→    try:
    35→        return asyncio.run(coro)
    36→    except Exception as e:
    37→        logger.debug(f"Guardrail check failed (non-blocking): {e}")
    38→        return None
    39→
    40→
    41→def check_input_guardrails(

    58→    if not executor.guardrail_pipeline:

    61→    guard_context = {

    72→        logger.warning("Task input blocked: %s", input_check.reason)
    73→        return False, error_msg, metrics
    74→
    75→    return True, "", {}

     1→"""Compound executor with vault-integrated knowledge persistence.
     2→
     3→Orchestrates execution lifecycle:
     4→  1. Query vault for experience guidance (prior similar runs)
     5→  2. Execute task with guidance
     6→  3. Log execution trajectory, decisions, metrics to vault
     7→  4. Extract reusable patterns for future runs
     8→"""

    38→def _run_async_guardrail(coro: Any) -> Any:
    39→    """Execute async guardrail check in sync context.
    40→
    41→    Non-blocking on failure - logs and returns None.

    43→    Args:
    44→        coro: Async coroutine to execute
    45→
    46→    Returns:

    53→        return None
    54→
    55→
    56→@dataclass
    57→class ExecutionResult:
    58→    """Result of a compound execution."""
    59→
    60→    success: bool
    61→    output: str
    62→    metrics: dict[str, Any]
    63→    duration_seconds: float
    64→    vault_experiment_path: str = ""
    65→    vault_decision_paths: list[str] | None = None
    66→    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    67→
    68→
    69→class CompoundExecutor:
    70→    """Executor for compound engineering tasks with vault integration.
    71→
    72→    Lifecycle:
    73→      1. get_experience_guidance() - Query vault for similar tasks
    74→      2. execute_task() - Run the task with token-efficient client
    75→      3. Logs are persisted to vault automatically
    76→      4. extract_patterns() - Save reusable insights
    77→
    78→    Optionally uses TokenEfficientClient for token-efficient LLM operations:
    79→      - SHA-256 caching eliminates redundant API calls
    80→      - Batch processing (Phase 1 cache + Phase 2 parallel) saves tokens
    81→      - Token metrics captured in ExecutionResult for compound scoring
    82→    """
    83→
    84→    def __init__(
    85→        self,
    86→        mcp_client: MCPClient,
    87→        token_client: Any | None = None,
    88→        guardrail_pipeline: GuardrailPipeline | None = None,
    89→        enable_guardrails: bool = True,
    90→        inflection_detector: Any | None = None,
    91→        skill_refiner: Any | None = None,
    92→        enable_skill_refinement: bool = True,
    93→        metrics_collector: Any | None = None,
    94→        journey_tracker: Any | None = None,
    95→        journey_persistence: Any | None = None,
    96→        alignment_analyzer: Any | None = None,
    97→        enable_alignment_analysis: bool = False,
    98→        degradation_detector: Any | None = None,
    99→        model_quality_classifier: Any | None = None,
   100→        retrospection_engine: Any | None = None,
   101→        universe_bridge: Any | None = None,
   102→    ):
   103→        """Initialize compound executor.
   104→
   105→        Args:
   106→            mcp_client: Connected MCPClient for vault operations
   107→            token_client: Optional TokenEfficientClient for LLM operations
   108→                If provided, enables token-efficient caching and batching
   109→            guardrail_pipeline: Optional GuardrailPipeline for safety checks.
   110→                If None and enable_guardrails is True, creates default pipeline.
   111→                If None and enable_guardrails is False, no guardrails applied.
   112→            enable_guardrails: If True (default), enable guardrails via default
   113→                pipeline (unless guardrail_pipeline is provided).
   114→            inflection_detector: Optional InflectionDetector for anomaly detection.
   115→                If None, creates default detector automatically.
   116→            skill_refiner: Optional SkillRefiner for learning from executions.
   117→                If None and enable_skill_refinement is True, creates default.
   118→            enable_skill_refinement: If True (default), enable skill refinement.
   119→            metrics_collector: Optional CompoundMetricsCollector for recording
   120→                execution metrics. If None, no metrics recorded.
   121→            journey_tracker: Optional JourneyTracker for 12D FLUME trajectory
   122→                tracking. If None, no journey tracking.
   123→            journey_persistence: Optional JourneyPersistence for persisting
   124→                journey data. Requires journey_tracker to be set.
   125→            alignment_analyzer: Optional RequestAlignmentAnalyzer for request
   126→                alignment analysis. If None and enable_alignment_analysis is True,
   127→                creates default analyzer.
   128→            enable_alignment_analysis: If True, enable alignment analysis
   129→                (requires alignment_analyzer or auto-creates one).
   130→            degradation_detector: Optional DegradationDetector for monitoring
   131→                metric drops (cache hit rate, token efficiency, coherence).
   132→                If None, no degradation detection.
   133→            model_quality_classifier: Optional ModelQualityClassifier for
   134→                predicting model failure likelihood. If None, no quality
   135→                classification.
   136→            retrospection_engine: Optional RetrospectionEngine for analyzing
   137→                live execution results and gating skill refinement.
   138→                If None, skill refinement is not gated by retrospection.
   139→            universe_bridge: Optional UniverseBridge for connecting journeys
   140→                to the universe simulation engine. If None, no universe tracking.
   141→        """
   142→        self.mcp_client = mcp_client
   143→        self.token_client = token_client
   144→        self._guardrail_pipeline = guardrail_pipeline
   145→        self._enable_guardrails = enable_guardrails
   146→        self._skill_refiner = skill_refiner
   147→        self._enable_skill_refinement = enable_skill_refinement
   148→        self._metrics_collector = metrics_collector
   149→        self._journey_tracker = journey_tracker
   150→        self._journey_persistence = journey_persistence

   889→    def _compute_token_delta(
   890→        self,
   891→        metrics_before: dict[str, Any] | None,
   892→        metrics_after: dict[str, Any],
   893→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   894→        """Compute token metric deltas (tokens used in this execution).
   895→
   896→        Args:
   897→            metrics_before: Token metrics before execution
   898→            metrics_after: Token metrics after execution
   899→
   900→        Returns:
   901→            Dict with token delta information
   902→        """
   903→        if not metrics_before:
   904→            return metrics_after
   905→
   906→        delta: dict[str, Any] = {}
   907→
   908→        if "total_tokens" in metrics_after and "total_tokens" in metrics_before:
   909→            delta["tokens_used"] = (
   910→                metrics_after["total_tokens"] - metrics_before["total_tokens"]
   911→            )
   912→        if "api_calls" in metrics_after and "api_calls" in metrics_before:
   913→            delta["api_calls_made"] = (
   914→                metrics_after["api_calls"] - metrics_before["api_calls"]
   915→            )
   916→        if "cache_hits" in metrics_after and "cache_hits" in metrics_before:
   917→            delta["cache_hits"] = (
   918→                metrics_after["cache_hits"] - metrics_before["cache_hits"]
   919→            )

   126→def detect_anomalies(
   127→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   128→    result: "ExecutionResult",
   129→    skill_name: str,
   130→    task_description: str,
   131→    project: str,
   132→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[str]]:
   133→    """Step 5: Detect execution anomalies.
   134→
   135→    Args:
   136→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   137→        result: Temporary execution result
   138→        skill_name: Skill name
   139→        task_description: Task description
   140→        project: Project name
   141→
   142→    Returns:
   143→        Tuple of (metrics_updates, decision_paths)
   144→    """
   145→    metrics_updates = {}
   146→    decision_paths = []
   147→
   148→    try:
   149→        from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity
   150→
   151→        anomaly = executor.inflection_detector.detect_anomaly(result)
   152→        metrics_updates["anomaly_severity"] = anomaly.severity.value
   153→        metrics_updates["anomaly_score"] = anomaly.score
   154→        logger.debug(
   155→            "Anomaly detection: severity=%s, score=%.2f, issues=%s",
   156→            anomaly.severity.value,
   157→            anomaly.score,
   158→            anomaly.issues,
   159→        )
   160→
   161→        if anomaly.severity == Severity.CRITICAL:
   162→            logger.warning(
   163→                "Critical inflection point detected: %s issues",
   164→                len(anomaly.issues),
   165→            )
   166→            try:
   167→                recommendation = (
   168→                    anomaly.recommendations[0]
   169→                    if anomaly.recommendations
   170→                    else "Investigate issues"
   171→                )
   172→                decision_path = executor.log_inflection_point(
   173→                    title=f"Critical anomaly in {skill_name}",
   174→                    context=(
   175→                        f"Task: {task_description}\nIssues: {'; '.join(anomaly.issues)}"
   176→                    ),
   177→                    decision="Re-execution recommended",
   178→                    rationale=f"Quality score {anomaly.score:.2f}, {recommendation}",
   179→                    project=project,
   180→                )
   181→                if decision_path:
   182→                    decision_paths.append(decision_path)
   183→            except Exception as e:
   184→                logger.debug("Failed to log inflection point (non-blocking): %s", e)
   185→    except Exception as e:
   186→        logger.debug("Anomaly detection failed (non-blocking): %s", e, exc_info=True)
   187→
   188→    return metrics_updates, decision_paths

   474→

   525→                        )

   527→            except Exception as e:

   367→                )

     1→"""Execution pipeline steps for CompoundExecutor.
     2→
     3→Individual step functions extracted from the 11-step execution pipeline.
     4→Each step is focused and testable in isolation.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import asyncio
     8→import json
     9→import logging
    10→import time
    11→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    12→
    13→from cohezion.compound.executor_types import ExecutionResult
    14→from cohezion.security.guardrail_pipeline import GuardrailAction
    15→
    16→
    17→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    18→    from cohezion.compound.executor import CompoundExecutor
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→
    23→def run_async_guardrail(coro: Any) -> Any:
    24→    """Execute async guardrail check in sync context.
    25→
    26→    Non-blocking on failure - logs and returns None.
    27→
    28→    Args:
    29→        coro: Async coroutine to execute
    30→
    31→    Returns:
    32→        Result of coroutine or None on failure
    33→    """
    34→    try:
    35→        return asyncio.run(coro)
    36→    except Exception as e:
    37→        logger.debug(f"Guardrail check failed (non-blocking): {e}")
    38→        return None
    39→
    40→
    41→def check_input_guardrails(
    42→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
    43→    task_description: str,
    44→    skill_name: str,
    45→    operation_type: str,
    46→) -> tuple[bool, str, dict[str, Any]]:
    47→    """Step 3: Check input via guardrails.
    48→
    49→    Args:
    50→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
    51→        task_description: Task description
    52→        skill_name: Skill name
    53→        operation_type: Operation type
    54→
    55→    Returns:
    56→        Tuple of (should_continue, error_msg, metrics)
    57→    """
    58→    if not executor.guardrail_pipeline:
    59→        return True, "", {}
    60→
    61→    guard_context = {
    62→        "skill_name": skill_name,
    63→        "operation_type": operation_type,
    64→        "task_description": task_description,
    65→    }
    66→    input_check = run_async_guardrail(
    67→        executor.guardrail_pipeline.check_input(task_description, guard_context)
    68→    )
    69→    if input_check and input_check.action == GuardrailAction.BLOCK:
    70→        error_msg = f"Input blocked by guardrails: {input_check.reason}"
    71→        metrics = {"error": error_msg, "blocked_by_guardrails": True}
    72→        logger.warning("Task input blocked: %s", input_check.reason)
    73→        return False, error_msg, metrics
    74→
    75→    return True, "", {}
    76→
    77→
    78→def check_output_guardrails(
    79→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
    80→    output: str,
    81→    skill_name: str,
    82→    operation_type: str,
    83→    task_description: str,
    84→) -> tuple[str, bool, dict[str, Any]]:
    85→    """Step 5: Check output via guardrails.
    86→
    87→    Args:
    88→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
    89→        output: Execution output
    90→        skill_name: Skill name
    91→        operation_type: Operation type
    92→        task_description: Task description
    93→
    94→    Returns:
    95→        Tuple of (modified_output, still_successful, metrics_updates)
    96→    """
    97→    if not executor.guardrail_pipeline:
    98→        return output, True, {}
    99→
   100→    guard_context = {
   101→        "skill_name": skill_name,
   102→        "operation_type": operation_type,
   103→        "task_description": task_description,
   104→    }
   105→    output_check = run_async_guardrail(
   106→        executor.guardrail_pipeline.check_output(output, guard_context)
   107→    )
   108→    if not output_check:
   109→        return output, True, {}
   110→
   111→    if output_check.action == GuardrailAction.BLOCK:
   112→        modified_output = "[Output blocked by content filter]"
   113→        metrics = {"output_blocked_by_guardrails": True}
   114→        logger.warning("Task output blocked: %s", output_check.reason)
   115→        return modified_output, False, metrics
   116→
   117→    if output_check.action == GuardrailAction.SANITIZE and output_check.modified_input:
   118→        modified_output = output_check.modified_input
   119→        metrics = {"output_sanitized_by_guardrails": True}
   120→        logger.debug("Task output sanitized")
   121→        return modified_output, True, metrics
   122→
   123→    return output, True, {}
   124→
   125→

   301→    task_description: str,
   302→    project: str,
   303→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[str], bool]:
   304→    """Step 7.5: Check for degradation and manage HIHO band.
   305→
   306→    Args:
   307→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   308→        coherence_val: Computed coherence value
   309→        duration_seconds: Execution duration
   310→        success: Whether execution succeeded
   311→        token_metrics: Token usage metrics
   312→        task_description: Task description
   313→        project: Project name
   314→
   315→    Returns:
   316→        Tuple of (metrics_updates, decision_paths, degradation_mode_flag)
   317→    """
   318→    metrics_updates = {}
   319→    decision_paths = []
   320→    degradation_mode = executor._degradation_mode
   321→
   322→    if 0.4 <= coherence_val <= 0.6 and degradation_mode:
   323→        logger.info(
   324→            "Cohesion returned to HIHO band (%.2f), exiting degradation mode",
   325→            coherence_val,
   326→        )
   327→        degradation_mode = False
   328→
   329→    if not executor._degradation_detector:
   330→        return metrics_updates, decision_paths, degradation_mode
   331→
   332→    try:
   333→        degradation_metrics = {
   334→            "combined_hit_rate": 0.0,
   335→            "tokens_per_second": 0.0,
   336→            "mean_coherence": coherence_val,
   337→            "elapsed_seconds": duration_seconds,
   338→            "success_rate": 1.0 if success else 0.0,
   339→        }
   340→        if token_metrics:
   341→            degradation_metrics["combined_hit_rate"] = token_metrics.get(
   342→                "cache_hit_rate", token_metrics.get("combined_hit_rate", 0.0)
   343→            )
   344→            degradation_metrics["tokens_per_second"] = token_metrics.get(
   345→                "tokens_per_second", 0.0
   346→            )
   347→
   348→        alerts = executor._degradation_detector.check_degradation(degradation_metrics)
   349→        if alerts:
   350→            metrics_updates["degradation_alerts"] = len(alerts)
   351→            for alert in alerts:
   352→                logger.warning(
   353→                    "Degradation alert [%s]: %s",
   354→                    alert.severity.value,
   355→                    alert.message,
   356→                )
   357→
   358→            critical_alerts = [a for a in alerts if a.severity.value == "CRITICAL"]
   359→            if critical_alerts:
   360→                degradation_mode = True
   361→                metrics_updates["execution_degraded"] = True
   362→                logger.warning(
   363→                    "Entering degradation mode: %d CRITICAL alerts, "
   364→                    "cohesion=%.2f outside HIHO band",
   365→                    len(critical_alerts),
   366→                    coherence_val,
   367→                )
   368→
   369→            for alert in critical_alerts:
   370→                try:
   371→                    dp = executor.log_inflection_point(
   372→                        title=f"Degradation: {alert.metric}",
   373→                        context=f"Task: {task_description}\n{alert.message}",
   374→                        decision="Investigate degradation",
   375→                        rationale=f"Current: {alert.current_value:.3f}, "
   376→                        f"Baseline: {alert.baseline_value:.3f}, "
   377→                        f"Threshold: {alert.threshold:.3f}",
   378→                        project=project,
   379→                    )
   380→                    if dp:
   381→                        decision_paths.append(dp)
   382→                except Exception as e:
   383→                    logger.debug(
   384→                        "Failed to log degradation alert (non-blocking): %s",
   385→                        e,
   386→                    )
   387→    except Exception as e:
   388→        logger.debug("Degradation detection failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   389→
   390→    return metrics_updates, decision_paths, degradation_mode
   391→
   392→
   393→def record_model_quality(
   394→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   395→    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None,
   396→    coherence: float,
   397→    success: bool,
   398→    duration_seconds: float,
   399→) -> None:
   400→    """Step 7.7: Record model quality metrics.
   401→
   402→    Args:
   403→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   404→        token_metrics: Token usage metrics
   405→        coherence: Computed coherence value
   406→        success: Whether execution succeeded
   407→        duration_seconds: Execution duration
   408→    """
   409→    if not executor._model_quality_classifier:
   410→        return
   411→
   412→    try:
   413→        model_name = "unknown"
   414→        tokens_used = 0
   415→        if token_metrics:
   416→            model_name = token_metrics.get("model", "unknown")
   417→            tokens_used = token_metrics.get("tokens_used", 0)
   418→
   419→        executor._model_quality_classifier.add_execution(
   420→            model=model_name,
   421→            coherence=coherence,
   422→            success=success,
   423→            tokens_used=tokens_used,
   424→            duration=duration_seconds,
   425→        )
   426→    except Exception as e:
   427→        logger.debug("Model quality recording failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   428→
   429→
   430→def record_metrics(
   431→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   432→    skill_name: str,
   433→    success: bool,
   434→    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None,
   435→    duration_seconds: float,
   436→) -> None:
   437→    """Step 8: Record execution metrics.
   438→
   439→    Args:
   440→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   441→        skill_name: Skill name
   442→        success: Whether execution succeeded
   443→        token_metrics: Token usage metrics
   444→        duration_seconds: Execution duration
   445→    """
   446→    if not executor._metrics_collector:
   447→        return
   448→
   449→    try:
   450→        tokens_used = 0
   451→        model_used = ""
   452→        if token_metrics:
   453→            tokens_used = token_metrics.get("tokens_used", 0)
   454→            model_used = token_metrics.get("model", "")
   455→
   456→        executor._metrics_collector.record_execution(
   457→            skill_name=skill_name,
   458→            success=success,
   459→            tokens_used=tokens_used,
   460→            duration_ms=duration_seconds * 1000,
   461→            model_used=model_used,
   462→        )
   463→    except Exception as e:
   464→        logger.debug("Metrics recording failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   465→
   466→
   467→def track_journey(
   468→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   469→    result: "ExecutionResult",
   470→    task_description: str,
   471→    operation_type: str,
   472→) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:
   473→    """Step 9: Track journey through 12D FLUME space.
   474→
   475→    Args:
   476→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   477→        result: Execution result
   478→        task_description: Task description
   479→        operation_type: Operation type
   480→
   481→    Returns:
   482→        Tuple of (point_tracked, metrics_updates)
   483→    """
   484→    metrics_updates = {}
   485→    point_tracked = False
   486→
   487→    if not executor._journey_tracker:
   488→        return point_tracked, metrics_updates
   489→
   490→    try:
   491→        point = executor._journey_tracker.track_execution(
   492→            result, task_description, operation_type
   493→        )
   494→        point_tracked = True
   495→
   496→        if point and point.metadata:
   497→            metrics_updates["phi_score"] = point.metadata.get("phi_score", 0.0)
   498→
   499→        if executor._journey_persistence and point:
   500→            try:
   501→                point_data = {
   502→                    "coherence": point.coherence,
   503→                    "efficiency": point.efficiency,
   504→                    "operation_type": point.operation_type,
   505→                    "task_description": point.task_description[:200],
   506→                    "timestamp": point.timestamp,
   507→                }
   508→                if point.metadata:
   509→                    point_data["metadata"] = point.metadata
   510→
   511→                exec_id = f"exec_{int(time.time())}"
   512→                try:
   513→                    asyncio.get_running_loop()
   514→                    _task = asyncio.ensure_future(  # noqa: RUF006
   515→                        executor._journey_persistence.save_trajectory_point(
   516→                            exec_id,
   517→                            point_data,
   518→                        )
   519→                    )
   520→                except RuntimeError:
   521→                    asyncio.run(
   522→                        executor._journey_persistence.save_trajectory_point(
   523→                            exec_id,
   524→                            point_data,
   525→                        )
   526→                    )
   527→            except Exception as e:
   528→                logger.debug("Journey persistence failed (non-blocking): %s", e)
   529→    except Exception as e:

   126→def detect_anomalies(
   127→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   128→    result: "ExecutionResult",
   129→    skill_name: str,
   130→    task_description: str,
   131→    project: str,
   132→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[str]]:
   133→    """Step 5: Detect execution anomalies.
   134→
   135→    Args:
   136→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   137→        result: Temporary execution result
   138→        skill_name: Skill name
   139→        task_description: Task description
   140→        project: Project name
   141→
   142→    Returns:
   143→        Tuple of (metrics_updates, decision_paths)
   144→    """
   145→    metrics_updates = {}
   146→    decision_paths = []
   147→
   148→    try:
   149→        from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity
   150→
   151→        anomaly = executor.inflection_detector.detect_anomaly(result)
   152→        metrics_updates["anomaly_severity"] = anomaly.severity.value
   153→        metrics_updates["anomaly_score"] = anomaly.score
   154→        logger.debug(
   155→            "Anomaly detection: severity=%s, score=%.2f, issues=%s",
   156→            anomaly.severity.value,
   157→            anomaly.score,
   158→            anomaly.issues,
   159→        )
   160→
   161→        if anomaly.severity == Severity.CRITICAL:
   162→            logger.warning(
   163→                "Critical inflection point detected: %s issues",
   164→                len(anomaly.issues),
   165→            )
   166→            try:
   167→                recommendation = (
   168→                    anomaly.recommendations[0]
   169→                    if anomaly.recommendations
   170→                    else "Investigate issues"
   171→                )
   172→                decision_path = executor.log_inflection_point(
   173→                    title=f"Critical anomaly in {skill_name}",
   174→                    context=(
   175→                        f"Task: {task_description}\nIssues: {'; '.join(anomaly.issues)}"
   176→                    ),
   177→                    decision="Re-execution recommended",
   178→                    rationale=f"Quality score {anomaly.score:.2f}, {recommendation}",
   179→                    project=project,
   180→                )
   181→                if decision_path:
   182→                    decision_paths.append(decision_path)
   183→            except Exception as e:
   184→                logger.debug("Failed to log inflection point (non-blocking): %s", e)
   185→    except Exception as e:
   186→        logger.debug("Anomaly detection failed (non-blocking): %s", e, exc_info=True)
   187→
   188→    return metrics_updates, decision_paths
   189→
   190→
   191→def extract_patterns(
   192→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   193→    success: bool,
   194→    experiment_path: str,
   195→    skill_name: str,
   196→    operation_type: str,
   197→    task_description: str,
   198→    metrics: dict[str, Any],
   199→) -> list[str]:
   200→    """Step 6: Extract execution patterns to vault.
   201→
   202→    Args:
   203→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   204→        success: Whether execution succeeded
   205→        experiment_path: Vault experiment path
   206→        skill_name: Skill name
   207→        operation_type: Operation type
   208→        task_description: Task description
   209→        metrics: Execution metrics
   210→
   211→    Returns:
   212→        List of decision paths created
   213→    """
   214→    decision_paths = []
   215→
   216→    if not success or not experiment_path or executor._degradation_mode:
   217→        return decision_paths
   218→
   219→    try:
   220→        pattern_path = executor.logger.extract_execution_pattern(
   221→            source_path=experiment_path,
   222→            pattern_name=f"{skill_name}_{operation_type}_success",
   223→            description=f"Successful execution pattern for {skill_name} "
   224→            f"operation: {operation_type}. "
   225→            f"Task: {task_description[:100]}",
   226→            code_example=f"Result metrics: {json.dumps(metrics, indent=2)}",
   227→            domain="compound-engineering",
   228→        )
   229→        if pattern_path:
   230→            decision_paths.append(pattern_path)
   231→    except Exception as e:
   232→        logger.warning("Failed to extract pattern: %s", e, exc_info=True)
   233→
   234→    return decision_paths
   235→
   236→
   237→def refine_skills(
   238→    executor: "CompoundExecutor",
   239→    success: bool,
   240→    skill_name: str,
   241→    operation_type: str,
   242→    output: str,
   243→    metrics: dict[str, Any],
   244→    duration_seconds: float,
   245→    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None,
   246→    decision_paths: list[str],
   247→    should_refine: bool,
   248→) -> list[str]:
   249→    """Step 7: Refine skills based on execution results.
   250→
   251→    Args:
   252→        executor: CompoundExecutor instance
   253→        success: Whether execution succeeded
   254→        skill_name: Skill name
   255→        operation_type: Operation type
   256→        output: Execution output
   257→        metrics: Execution metrics
   258→        duration_seconds: Execution duration
   259→        token_metrics: Token usage metrics
   260→        decision_paths: Paths created so far
   261→        should_refine: Whether retrospection allows refinement
   262→
   263→    Returns:
   264→        Updated list of decision paths
   265→    """
   266→    if not (success and executor.skill_refiner and should_refine):
   267→        return decision_paths
   268→
   269→    try:
   270→        exec_result = {
   271→            "success": success,
   272→            "output": output,
   273→            "metrics": metrics,
   274→            "duration_seconds": duration_seconds,
   275→            "token_metrics": token_metrics,
   276→        }
   277→
   278→        refined_path = executor.skill_refiner.refine(
   279→            skill_name=skill_name,
   280→            operation_type=operation_type,
   281→            execution_result=exec_result,
   282→            patterns_extracted=decision_paths,
   283→        )
   284→
   285→        if refined_path:
   286→            logger.info(f"Skill refined: {refined_path}")
   287→            decision_paths.append(refined_path)
   288→
   289→    except Exception as e:
   290→        logger.debug("Skill refinement failed (non-blocking): %s", e, exc_info=True)

{}\n    decision_paths = []\n\n    try:\n        from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity\n\n        anomaly = executor.inflection_detector.detect_anomaly(result)\n        metrics_updates[\"anomaly_severity\"] = anomaly.severity.value\n        metrics_updates[\"anomaly_score\"] = anomaly.score\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Anomaly detection: severity=%s, score=%.2f, issues=%s\",\n            anomaly.severity.value,\n            anomaly.score,\n            anomaly.issues,\n        )\n\n        if anomaly.severity == Severity.CRITICAL:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Critical inflection point detected: %s issues\",\n                len(anomaly.issues),\n            )\n            try:\n                recommendation = (\n                    anomaly.recommendations[0]\n                    if anomaly.recommendations\n                    else \"Investigate issues\"\n                )\n                decision_path = executor.log_inflection_point(\n                    title=f\"Critical anomaly in {skill_name}\",\n                    context=(\n                        f\"Task: {task_description}\\nIssues: {'; '.join(anomaly.issues)}\"\n                    ),\n                    decision=\"Re-execution recommended\",\n                    rationale=f\"Quality score {anomaly.score:.2f}, {recommendation}\",\n                    project=project,\n                )\n                if decision_path:\n                    decision_paths.append(decision_path)\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Failed to log inflection point (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Anomaly detection failed (non-blocking): %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n\n    return metrics_updates, decision_paths\n\n\ndef extract_patterns(\n    executor: \"CompoundExecutor\",\n    success: bool,\n    experiment_path: str,\n    skill_name: str,\n    operation_type: str,\n    task_description: str,\n    metrics: dict[str, Any],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 6: Extract execution patterns to vault.\n\n    Args:\n        executor: CompoundExecutor instance\n        success: Whether execution succeeded\n        experiment_path: Vault experiment path\n        skill_name: Skill name\n        operation_type: Operation type\n        task_description: Task description\n        metrics: Execution metrics\n\n    Returns:\n        List of decision paths created\n    \"\"\"\n    decision_paths = []\n\n    if not success or not experiment_path or executor._degradation_mode:\n        return decision_paths\n\n    try:\n        pattern_path = executor.logger.extract_execution_pattern(\n            source_path=experiment_path,\n            pattern_name=f\"{skill_name}_{operation_type}_success\",\n            description=f\"Successful execution pattern for {skill_name} \"\n            f\"operation: {operation_type}. \"\n            f\"Task: {task_description[:100]}\",\n            code_example=f\"Result metrics: {json.dumps(metrics, indent=2)}\",\n            domain=\"compound-engineering\",\n        )\n        if pattern_path:\n            decision_paths.append(pattern_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Failed to extract pattern: %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n\n    return decision_paths\n\n\ndef refine_skills(\n    executor: \"CompoundExecutor\",\n    success: bool,\n    skill_name: str,\n    operation_type: str,\n    output: str,\n    metrics: dict[str, Any],\n    duration_seconds: float,\n    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None,\n    decision_paths: list[str],\n    should_refine: bool,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 7: Refine skills based on execution results.\n\n    Args:\n        executor: CompoundExecutor instance\n        success: Whether execution succeeded\n        skill_name: Skill name\n        operation_type: Operation type\n        output: Execution output\n        metrics: Execution metrics\n        duration_seconds: Execution duration\n        token_metrics: Token usage metrics\n        decision_paths: Paths created so far\n        should_refine: Whether retrospection allows refinement\n\n    Returns:\n        Updated list of decision paths\n    \"\"\"\n    if not (success and executor.skill_refiner and should_refine):\n        return decision_paths\n\n    try:\n        exec_result = {\n            \"success\": success,\n            \"output\": output,\n            \"metrics\": metrics,\n            \"duration_seconds\": duration_seconds,\n            \"token_metrics\": token_metrics,\n        }\n\n        refined_path = executor.skill_refiner.refine(\n            skill_name=skill_name,\n            operation_type=operation_type,\n            execution_result=exec_result,\n            patterns_extracted=decision_paths,\n        )\n\n        if refined_path:\n            logger.info(f\"Skill refined: {refined_path}\")\n            decision_paths.append(refined_path)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Skill refinement failed (non-blocking): %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n\n    return decision_paths\n"
}

3. **Verified all tests pass**: 31 executor tests passing after refactoring

    59→class CircuitBreaker:
    60→    """Circuit breaker for managing model availability with recovery.
    61→
    62→    States:
    63→    - CLOSED: Normal operation, requests go through
    64→    - OPEN: Too many errors, requests rejected
    65→    - HALF_OPEN: Testing if model recovered
    66→
    67→    Configuration:
    68→    - error_threshold: Consecutive errors before OPEN (default: 3)
    69→    - recovery_timeout_sec: Seconds before HALF_OPEN test (default: 300s)
    70→    - error_rate_threshold: Error rate threshold for OPEN (default: 50%)
    71→    """
    72→
    73→    def __init__(
    74→        self,
    75→        model: str,
    76→        error_threshold: int = 3,
    77→        success_threshold: int = 5,
    78→        recovery_timeout_sec: float = 300.0,
    79→        error_rate_threshold: float = 0.50,
    80→        latency_threshold_ms: float = 5000.0,
    81→    ):
    82→        """Initialize circuit breaker.
    83→
    84→        Args:
    85→            model: Model name
    86→            error_threshold: Consecutive errors before OPEN (default: 3)
    87→            success_threshold: Consecutive successes before CLOSED (default: 5)
    88→            recovery_timeout_sec: Seconds before HALF_OPEN test (default: 300s)
    89→            error_rate_threshold: Error rate threshold for OPEN (default: 0.50)
    90→            latency_threshold_ms: Latency threshold for triggering open (default: 5000ms)
    91→        """
    92→        self.model = model
    93→        self.error_threshold = error_threshold
    94→        self.success_threshold = success_threshold
    95→        self.recovery_timeout_sec = recovery_timeout_sec
    96→        self.error_rate_threshold = error_rate_threshold
    97→        self.latency_threshold_ms = latency_threshold_ms
    98→
    99→        self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   100→        self.metrics = ModelHealthMetrics(model=model)
   101→        self.opened_at: float | None = None
   102→
   103→    def record_success(self, latency_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
   104→        """Record successful execution.
   105→
   106→        Args:
   107→            latency_ms: Response latency in milliseconds
   108→        """
   109→        self.metrics.error_count = 0
   110→        self.metrics.success_count += 1
   111→        self.metrics.last_success_time = time.time()
   112→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   113→        self.metrics.last_latency_ms = latency_ms
   114→
   115→        if self.metrics.avg_latency_ms == 0.0:
   116→            self.metrics.avg_latency_ms = latency_ms
   117→        else:
   118→            self.metrics.avg_latency_ms = (
   119→                0.7 * self.metrics.avg_latency_ms + 0.3 * latency_ms
   120→            )
   121→
   122→        if self.state == CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   123→            if self.metrics.success_count >= self.success_threshold:
   124→                self._transition_to_closed()
   125→
   126→    def record_error(self, latency_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
   127→        """Record failed execution.
   128→
   129→        Args:
   130→            latency_ms: Latency before failure (for tracking slow failures)
   131→        """
   132→        self.metrics.error_count += 1
   133→        self.metrics.success_count = 0
   134→        self.metrics.last_error_time = time.time()
   135→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   136→        self.metrics.total_errors += 1
   137→        self.metrics.last_latency_ms = latency_ms
   138→
   139→        if self.metrics.error_count >= self.error_threshold:
   140→            logger.warning(
   141→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   142→                f"{self.metrics.error_count} consecutive errors threshold reached"
   143→            )
   144→            self._transition_to_open()
   145→
   146→        if (
   147→            self.metrics.total_requests >= 10
   148→            and self.metrics.error_rate >= self.error_rate_threshold
   149→        ):
   150→            logger.warning(
   151→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   152→                f"error rate {self.metrics.error_rate:.1%} exceeds threshold"
   153→            )
   154→            self._transition_to_open()
   155→
   156→    def record_latency_spike(self) -> None:
   157→        """Record latency spike (slow response detected)."""
   158→        if self.metrics.last_latency_ms > self.latency_threshold_ms:
   159→            logger.warning(
   160→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   161→                f"latency spike {self.metrics.last_latency_ms:.0f}ms exceeds threshold"
   162→            )
   163→
   164→    def allow_request(self) -> bool:
   165→        """Check if request should be allowed.
   166→
   167→        Returns:
   168→            True if request allowed, False if circuit is open
   169→        """
   170→        if self.state == CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED:
   171→            return True
   172→        elif self.state == CircuitBreakerState.OPEN:
   173→            if self.opened_at:
   174→                elapsed = time.time() - self.opened_at
   175→                if elapsed >= self.recovery_timeout_sec:
   176→                    self._transition_to_half_open()
   177→                    return True
   178→            return False
   179→        elif self.state == CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   180→            return True
   181→        else:
   182→            return False
   183→
   184→    def _transition_to_open(self) -> None:
   185→        """Transition to OPEN state."""
   186→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.OPEN:
   187→            logger.warning(
   188→                f"Circuit breaker OPEN for {self.model}: "
   189→                f"error_count={self.metrics.error_count}, "
   190→                f"error_rate={self.metrics.error_rate:.1%}"
   191→            )
   192→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.OPEN
   193→            self.opened_at = time.time()
   194→
   195→    def _transition_to_half_open(self) -> None:
   196→        """Transition to HALF_OPEN state (testing recovery)."""
   197→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   198→            logger.info(
   199→                f"Circuit breaker HALF_OPEN for {self.model}: "
   200→                f"testing recovery after {self.recovery_timeout_sec}s"
   201→            )
   202→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN
   203→            self.metrics.error_count = 0
   204→            self.metrics.success_count = 0
   205→
   206→    def _transition_to_closed(self) -> None:
   207→        """Transition to CLOSED state (recovered)."""
   208→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED:
   209→            downtime_sec = time.time() - (self.opened_at or 0)
   210→            logger.info(
   211→                f"Circuit breaker CLOSED for {self.model}: recovered "
   212→                f"after {downtime_sec:.1f}s downtime"
   213→            )
   214→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   215→            self.opened_at = None
   216→            self.metrics.error_count = 0
   217→            self.metrics.success_count = 0
   218→
   219→    def reset(self) -> None:
   220→        """Reset circuit breaker (testing only)."""
   221→        self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   222→        self.opened_at = None
   223→        self.metrics = ModelHealthMetrics(model=self.model)
   224→

     1→"""Graceful fallback strategy with circuit breaker pattern and cost preservation.
     2→
     3→Features:
     4→- Intelligent fallback chain: primary → secondary → emergency
     5→- Circuit breaker pattern with three states: CLOSED, OPEN, HALF_OPEN
     6→- Model health tracking (latency, error rate)
     7→- Cost-aware fallback selection (prefer cheapest available)
     8→- Non-blocking design (graceful degradation)
     9→- Comprehensive logging and metrics
    10→
    11→Architecture:
    12→  Execution Request
    13→       ↓
    14→  Check Circuit Breaker Status
    15→       ├→ CLOSED: Use model
    16→       ├→ HALF_OPEN: Allow probe request
    17→       └→ OPEN: Fallback
    18→       ↓
    19→  Select Fallback Model
    20→       ├→ Check cost (prefer cheaper if available)
    21→       ├→ Check quality loss acceptable
    22→       └→ Try chain: secondary → emergency
    23→       ↓
    24→  Execute with Selected Model
    25→       ↓
    26→  Record Metrics (health, cost, degradation)
    27→
    28→Cost Preservation Strategy:
    29→  1. Primary unavailable → Try secondary (potentially cheaper)
    30→  2. Secondary unavailable → Try emergency (most reliable)
    31→  3. All unavailable → Use cheapest fallback
    32→  4. Track cost savings during degradation periods
    33→
    34→Usage:
    35→    fallback = FallbackStrategy()
    36→    model, degraded, cost = fallback.execute_with_fallback(
    37→        primary_model="deepseek-r1:8b",
    38→        query="Complex analysis task",
    39→        available_models=["phi3:mini", "qwen3-coder:32b", "deepseek-r1:8b"],
    40→        cost_tracker=tracker
    41→    )
    42→    if degraded:
    43→        logger.info(f"Fallback to {model}, cost savings preserved")
    44→"""
    45→
    46→import logging
    47→import time
    48→
    49→from cohezion.swarm.fallback_types import (
    50→    CircuitBreakerState,
    51→    FallbackEvent,
    52→    ModelHealthMetrics,
    53→)
    54→
    55→
    56→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    57→
    58→
    59→class CircuitBreaker:
    60→    """Circuit breaker for managing model availability with recovery.

    59→class CircuitBreaker:
    60→    """Circuit breaker for managing model availability with recovery.
    61→
    62→    States:
    63→    - CLOSED: Normal operation, requests go through
    64→    - OPEN: Too many errors, requests rejected
    65→    - HALF_OPEN: Testing if model recovered
    66→
    67→    Configuration:
    68→    - error_threshold: Consecutive errors before OPEN (default: 3)
    69→    - recovery_timeout_sec: Seconds before HALF_OPEN test (default: 300s)
    70→    - error_rate_threshold: Error rate threshold for OPEN (default: 50%)
    71→    """
    72→
    73→    def __init__(
    74→        self,
    75→        model: str,
    76→        error_threshold: int = 3,
    77→        success_threshold: int = 5,
    78→        recovery_timeout_sec: float = 300.0,
    79→        error_rate_threshold: float = 0.50,
    80→        latency_threshold_ms: float = 5000.0,
    81→    ):
    82→        """Initialize circuit breaker.
    83→
    84→        Args:
    85→            model: Model name
    86→            error_threshold: Consecutive errors before OPEN (default: 3)
    87→            success_threshold: Consecutive successes before CLOSED (default: 5)
    88→            recovery_timeout_sec: Seconds before HALF_OPEN test (default: 300s)
    89→            error_rate_threshold: Error rate threshold for OPEN (default: 0.50)
    90→            latency_threshold_ms: Latency threshold for triggering open (default: 5000ms)
    91→        """
    92→        self.model = model
    93→        self.error_threshold = error_threshold
    94→        self.success_threshold = success_threshold
    95→        self.recovery_timeout_sec = recovery_timeout_sec
    96→        self.error_rate_threshold = error_rate_threshold
    97→        self.latency_threshold_ms = latency_threshold_ms
    98→
    99→        self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   100→        self.metrics = ModelHealthMetrics(model=model)
   101→        self.opened_at: float | None = None
   102→
   103→    def record_success(self, latency_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
   104→        """Record successful execution.
   105→
   106→        Args:
   107→            latency_ms: Response latency in milliseconds
   108→        """
   109→        self.metrics.error_count = 0
   110→        self.metrics.success_count += 1
   111→        self.metrics.last_success_time = time.time()
   112→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   113→        self.metrics.last_latency_ms = latency_ms
   114→
   115→        if self.metrics.avg_latency_ms == 0.0:
   116→            self.metrics.avg_latency_ms = latency_ms
   117→        else:
   118→            self.metrics.avg_latency_ms = (
   119→                0.7 * self.metrics.avg_latency_ms + 0.3 * latency_ms
   120→            )
   121→
   122→        if self.state == CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   123→            if self.metrics.success_count >= self.success_threshold:
   124→                self._transition_to_closed()
   125→
   126→    def record_error(self, latency_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
   127→        """Record failed execution.
   128→
   129→        Args:
   130→            latency_ms: Latency before failure (for tracking slow failures)
   131→        """
   132→        self.metrics.error_count += 1
   133→        self.metrics.success_count = 0
   134→        self.metrics.last_error_time = time.time()
   135→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   136→        self.metrics.total_errors += 1
   137→        self.metrics.last_latency_ms = latency_ms
   138→
   139→        if self.metrics.error_count >= self.error_threshold:
   140→            logger.warning(
   141→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   142→                f"{self.metrics.error_count} consecutive errors threshold reached"
   143→            )
   144→            self._transition_to_open()
   145→
   146→        if (
   147→            self.metrics.total_requests >= 10
   148→            and self.metrics.error_rate >= self.error_rate_threshold
   149→        ):
   150→            logger.warning(
   151→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   152→                f"error rate {self.metrics.error_rate:.1%} exceeds threshold"
   153→            )
   154→            self._transition_to_open()
   155→
   156→    def record_latency_spike(self) -> None:
   157→        """Record latency spike (slow response detected)."""
   158→        if self.metrics.last_latency_ms > self.latency_threshold_ms:
   159→            logger.warning(
   160→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   161→                f"latency spike {self.metrics.last_latency_ms:.0f}ms exceeds threshold"
   162→            )
   163→
   164→    def allow_request(self) -> bool:
   165→        """Check if request should be allowed.
   166→
   167→        Returns:
   168→            True if request allowed, False if circuit is open
   169→        """
   170→        if self.state == CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED:
   171→            return True
   172→        elif self.state == CircuitBreakerState.OPEN:
   173→            if self.opened_at:
   174→                elapsed = time.time() - self.opened_at
   175→                if elapsed >= self.recovery_timeout_sec:
   176→                    self._transition_to_half_open()
   177→                    return True
   178→            return False
   179→        elif self.state == CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   180→            return True
   181→        else:
   182→            return False
   183→
   184→    def _transition_to_open(self) -> None:
   185→        """Transition to OPEN state."""
   186→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.OPEN:
   187→            logger.warning(
   188→                f"Circuit breaker OPEN for {self.model}: "
   189→                f"error_count={self.metrics.error_count}, "
   190→                f"error_rate={self.metrics.error_rate:.1%}"
   191→            )
   192→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.OPEN
   193→            self.opened_at = time.time()
   194→
   195→    def _transition_to_half_open(self) -> None:
   196→        """Transition to HALF_OPEN state (testing recovery)."""
   197→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   198→            logger.info(
   199→                f"Circuit breaker HALF_OPEN for {self.model}: "
   200→                f"testing recovery after {self.recovery_timeout_sec}s"
   201→            )
   202→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN
   203→            self.metrics.error_count = 0
   204→            self.metrics.success_count = 0
   205→
   206→    def _transition_to_closed(self) -> None:
   207→        """Transition to CLOSED state (recovered)."""
   208→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED:
   209→            downtime_sec = time.time() - (self.opened_at or 0)
   210→            logger.info(
   211→                f"Circuit breaker CLOSED for {self.model}: recovered "
   212→                f"after {downtime_sec:.1f}s downtime"
   213→            )
   214→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   215→            self.opened_at = None
   216→            self.metrics.error_count = 0
   217→            self.metrics.success_count = 0
   218→
   219→    def reset(self) -> None:
   220→        """Reset circuit breaker (testing only)."""
   221→        self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   222→        self.opened_at = None
   223→        self.metrics = ModelHealthMetrics(model=self.model)
   224→

    56→class FallbackStrategy:
    57→    """Circuit breaker for managing model availability with recovery.
    58→
    59→    States:
    60→    - CLOSED: Normal operation, requests go through
    61→    - OPEN: Too many errors, requests rejected
    62→    - HALF_OPEN: Testing if model recovered
    63→
    64→    Configuration:
    65→    - error_threshold: Consecutive errors before OPEN (default: 3)
    66→    - recovery_timeout_sec: Seconds before HALF_OPEN test (default: 300s)
    67→    - error_rate_threshold: Error rate threshold for OPEN (default: 50%)
    68→    """
    69→
    70→    def __init__(
    71→        self,
    72→        model: str,
    73→        error_threshold: int = 3,
    74→        success_threshold: int = 5,
    75→        recovery_timeout_sec: float = 300.0,
    76→        error_rate_threshold: float = 0.50,
    77→        latency_threshold_ms: float = 5000.0,
    78→    ):
    79→        """Initialize circuit breaker.
    80→
    81→        Args:
    82→            model: Model name
    83→            error_threshold: Consecutive errors before OPEN (default: 3)
    84→            success_threshold: Consecutive successes before CLOSED (default: 5)
    85→            recovery_timeout_sec: Seconds before HALF_OPEN test (default: 300s)
    86→            error_rate_threshold: Error rate threshold for OPEN (default: 0.50)
    87→            latency_threshold_ms: Latency threshold for triggering open (default: 5000ms)
    88→        """
    89→        self.model = model
    90→        self.error_threshold = error_threshold
    91→        self.success_threshold = success_threshold
    92→        self.recovery_timeout_sec = recovery_timeout_sec
    93→        self.error_rate_threshold = error_rate_threshold
    94→        self.latency_threshold_ms = latency_threshold_ms
    95→
    96→        self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
    97→        self.metrics = ModelHealthMetrics(model=model)
    98→        self.opened_at: float | None = None
    99→
   100→    def record_success(self, latency_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
   101→        """Record successful execution.
   102→
   103→        Args:
   104→            latency_ms: Response latency in milliseconds
   105→        """
   106→        self.metrics.error_count = 0
   107→        self.metrics.success_count += 1
   108→        self.metrics.last_success_time = time.time()
   109→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   110→        self.metrics.last_latency_ms = latency_ms
   111→
   112→        if self.metrics.avg_latency_ms == 0.0:
   113→            self.metrics.avg_latency_ms = latency_ms
   114→        else:
   115→            self.metrics.avg_latency_ms = (
   116→                0.7 * self.metrics.avg_latency_ms + 0.3 * latency_ms
   117→            )
   118→
   119→        if self.state == CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   120→            if self.metrics.success_count >= self.success_threshold:
   121→                self._transition_to_closed()
   122→
   123→    def record_error(self, latency_ms: float = 0.0) -> None:
   124→        """Record failed execution.
   125→
   126→        Args:
   127→            latency_ms: Latency before failure (for tracking slow failures)
   128→        """
   129→        self.metrics.error_count += 1
   130→        self.metrics.success_count = 0
   131→        self.metrics.last_error_time = time.time()
   132→        self.metrics.total_requests += 1
   133→        self.metrics.total_errors += 1
   134→        self.metrics.last_latency_ms = latency_ms
   135→
   136→        if self.metrics.error_count >= self.error_threshold:
   137→            logger.warning(
   138→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   139→                f"{self.metrics.error_count} consecutive errors threshold reached"
   140→            )
   141→            self._transition_to_open()
   142→
   143→        if (
   144→            self.metrics.total_requests >= 10
   145→            and self.metrics.error_rate >= self.error_rate_threshold
   146→        ):
   147→            logger.warning(
   148→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   149→                f"error rate {self.metrics.error_rate:.1%} exceeds threshold"
   150→            )
   151→            self._transition_to_open()
   152→
   153→    def record_latency_spike(self) -> None:
   154→        """Record latency spike (slow response detected)."""
   155→        if self.metrics.last_latency_ms > self.latency_threshold_ms:
   156→            logger.warning(
   157→                f"Circuit breaker: {self.model} - "
   158→                f"latency spike {self.metrics.last_latency_ms:.0f}ms exceeds threshold"
   159→            )
   160→
   161→    def allow_request(self) -> bool:
   162→        """Check if request should be allowed.
   163→
   164→        Returns:
   165→            True if request allowed, False if circuit is open
   166→        """
   167→        if self.state == CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED:
   168→            return True
   169→        elif self.state == CircuitBreakerState.OPEN:
   170→            if self.opened_at:
   171→                elapsed = time.time() - self.opened_at
   172→                if elapsed >= self.recovery_timeout_sec:
   173→                    self._transition_to_half_open()
   174→                    return True
   175→            return False
   176→        elif self.state == CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   177→            return True
   178→        else:
   179→            return False
   180→
   181→    def _transition_to_open(self) -> None:
   182→        """Transition to OPEN state."""
   183→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.OPEN:
   184→            logger.warning(
   185→                f"Circuit breaker OPEN for {self.model}: "
   186→                f"error_count={self.metrics.error_count}, "
   187→                f"error_rate={self.metrics.error_rate:.1%}"
   188→            )
   189→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.OPEN
   190→            self.opened_at = time.time()
   191→
   192→    def _transition_to_half_open(self) -> None:
   193→        """Transition to HALF_OPEN state (testing recovery)."""
   194→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN:
   195→            logger.info(
   196→                f"Circuit breaker HALF_OPEN for {self.model}: "
   197→                f"testing recovery after {self.recovery_timeout_sec}s"
   198→            )
   199→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.HALF_OPEN
   200→            self.metrics.error_count = 0
   201→            self.metrics.success_count = 0
   202→
   203→    def _transition_to_closed(self) -> None:
   204→        """Transition to CLOSED state (recovered)."""
   205→        if self.state != CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED:
   206→            downtime_sec = time.time() - (self.opened_at or 0)
   207→            logger.info(
   208→                f"Circuit breaker CLOSED for {self.model}: recovered "
   209→                f"after {downtime_sec:.1f}s downtime"
   210→            )
   211→            self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   212→            self.opened_at = None
   213→            self.metrics.error_count = 0
   214→            self.metrics.success_count = 0
   215→
   216→    def reset(self) -> None:
   217→        """Reset circuit breaker (testing only)."""
   218→        self.state = CircuitBreakerState.CLOSED
   219→        self.opened_at = None
   220→        self.metrics = ModelHealthMetrics(model=self.model)
   221→
   222→
   223→class FallbackStrategy:
   224→    """Intelligent fallback strategy with circuit breaker and cost preservation.
   225→
   226→    Manages graceful degradation when models become unavailable:
   227→    1. Monitor model health (latency, error rate)
   228→    2. Detect unavailability via circuit breaker
